# Recognizer catalogue — legacy and fresh scopes

3322 training/reference questions, not independent evaluation. Contains spoilers; not player help.

Rows labeled q_fresh_ use a fresh v1.0 session. All other groups show retained legacy prototype dialogue and must not be counted as current playable answers. See IMPLEMENTATION_05_CONVERSATION_INVENTORY.json and IMPLEMENTATION_05_EVALUATION_AFTER.json for fresh coverage and independent questions.

## log vs live — FRESH v1.0

| Does reading a log restart a service? | A live service report describes observed operation at the recorded session time. A historical document records a dated claim. They can disagree without either record being silently rewritten. |
| What is a retained event? | A live service report describes observed operation at the recorded session time. A historical document records a dated claim. They can disagree without either record being silently rewritten. |
| Is this an observation or a document? | A live service report describes observed operation at the recorded session time. A historical document records a dated claim. They can disagree without either record being silently rewritten. |
| Can you trust old timestamps? | A live service report describes observed operation at the recorded session time. A historical document records a dated claim. They can disagree without either record being silently rewritten. |
| What does a shutdown report prove? | A live service report describes observed operation at the recorded session time. A historical document records a dated claim. They can disagree without either record being silently rewritten. |
| Are logs current? | A live service report describes observed operation at the recorded session time. A historical document records a dated claim. They can disagree without either record being silently rewritten. |
